===== .env.example =====

# my-site 环境变量示例
#
# 实际运行时，这些变量的值在 Cloudflare Worker 的
# "Variables and Secrets" 中配置，而不是 .env 文件。
# 本文件仅作参考，说明项目需要哪些变量。

# ============ 静态构建时使用（Astro build）============

# GitHub Pages 构建开关（true 时 output=static）
GITHUB_PAGES=true

# 前端调 API 的地址
PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL=https://my-site-n7j.pages.dev

# ============ Cloudflare Worker Secrets ============
# 通过 `wrangler secret put <NAME>` 或 Dashboard 配置

# Resend 邮件服务密钥（用于发送验证码）
RESEND_API_KEY=re_xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx

# OTP 签名密钥（64 位 hex，与 Resend 分开）
# 生成方式：openssl rand -hex 32
OTP_SECRET=xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx

# ============ Cloudflare Worker Vars ============
# 通过 wrangler.toml [vars] 或 Dashboard 配置

# Cap 服务公网 URL（Service Binding 失败时兜底）
CAP_WORKER_URL=https://cap-worker.ezuoju2.workers.dev

# ============ Cloudflare D1 / KV Bindings ============
# 这些在 wrangler.toml 中声明，不是环境变量

# DB: D1 数据库（my-site-db）
# SESSION: KV 命名空间
# CAP_WORKER: Service Binding 到 cap-worker


===== .github/workflows/ci.yml =====

name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
  pull_request:
    branches:
      - main
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  check:
    name: Type check and audit
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v6
        with:
          node-version: 22
          cache: npm

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Astro type check
        run: npx astro check
        continue-on-error: false

      - name: Build (GitHub Pages static mode)
        env:
          GITHUB_PAGES: "true"
          PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL: "https://my-site-n7j.pages.dev"
        run: |
          rm -rf src/pages/api
          rm -rf src/middleware.ts
          npm run build

      - name: npm audit (high and above, non-blocking)
        run: npm audit --audit-level=high
        continue-on-error: true

      - name: Done
        run: echo "CI checks completed."


===== .github/workflows/clear-users.yml =====

name: Clear All Users (Manual Only)

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      confirm:
        description: 'Type "YES-CLEAR-ALL" to confirm'
        required: true
        type: string

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  clear:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10

    env:
      CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
      CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}

    steps:
      - name: Verify confirmation string
        run: |
          set -e
          if [ "${{ inputs.confirm }}" != "YES-CLEAR-ALL" ]; then
            echo "::error::Confirmation string does not match. Aborting."
            exit 1
          fi
          echo "Confirmation string verified."

      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v6
        with:
          node-version: 22
          cache: npm

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Show current user count
        run: |
          npx wrangler d1 execute my-site-db --remote \
            --command="SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM users;"

      - name: Delete all users
        run: |
          npx wrangler d1 execute my-site-db --remote \
            --command="DELETE FROM users;"

      - name: Verify empty
        run: |
          npx wrangler d1 execute my-site-db --remote \
            --command="SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM users;"

      - name: Done
        run: echo "All users cleared."


===== .github/workflows/cloudflare-pages-audit.yml =====

name: Audit Cloudflare Pages

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  audit:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10

    env:
      CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_AUDIT_API_TOKEN }}
      CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}

    steps:
      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v6
        with:
          node-version: 22

      - name: Install Wrangler
        run: npm install --global wrangler@4.130.0

      - name: Audit Cloudflare Pages project configuration
        run: |
          set -eu

          echo "=== CLOUDFLARE PAGES PROJECT ==="

          node - <<'NODE'
          const accountId = process.env.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID;
          const token = process.env.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN;

          if (!accountId || !token) {
            throw new Error("Required Cloudflare environment variables are missing");
          }

          const url =
            `https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/${accountId}` +
            `/pages/projects/my-site`;

          const response = await fetch(url, {
            headers: {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
          });

          const payload = await response.json();

          if (!response.ok || !payload.success) {
            throw new Error(JSON.stringify(payload.errors ?? payload));
          }

          const p = payload.result ?? {};
          const production = p.deployment_configs?.production ?? {};
          const preview = p.deployment_configs?.preview ?? {};

          function keys(value) {
            if (!value || typeof value !== "object") {
              return [];
            }

            return Object.keys(value).sort();
          }

          function services(value) {
            if (!value || typeof value !== "object") {
              return {};
            }

            return Object.fromEntries(
              Object.entries(value)
                .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
                .map(([name, config]) => [
                  name,
                  {
                    service:
                      config && typeof config === "object"
                        ? config.service ?? null
                        : null,
                    environment:
                      config && typeof config === "object"
                        ? config.environment ?? null
                        : null,
                    entrypoint:
                      config && typeof config === "object"
                        ? config.entrypoint ?? null
                        : null,
                  },
                ]),
            );
          }

          function envVarTypes(value) {
            if (!value || typeof value !== "object") {
              return {};
            }

            return Object.fromEntries(
              Object.entries(value)
                .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
                .map(([name, config]) => [
                  name,
                  {
                    type:
                      config && typeof config === "object"
                        ? config.type ?? "unknown"
                        : "unknown",
                  },
                ]),
            );
          }

          function summarize(config) {
            return {
              compatibility_date: config.compatibility_date ?? null,
              compatibility_flags: config.compatibility_flags ?? [],

              env_vars: envVarTypes(config.env_vars),

              d1_databases: keys(config.d1_databases),
              kv_namespaces: keys(config.kv_namespaces),
              r2_buckets: keys(config.r2_buckets),

              services: services(config.services),

              durable_object_namespaces:
                keys(config.durable_object_namespaces),

              queue_producers:
                keys(config.queue_producers),

              vectorize_bindings:
                keys(config.vectorize_bindings),

              ai_bindings:
                keys(config.ai_bindings),

              analytics_engine_datasets:
                keys(config.analytics_engine_datasets),

              hyperdrive:
                keys(config.hyperdrive),

              placement: config.placement ?? null,
              usage_model: config.usage_model ?? null,
            };
          }

          console.log(JSON.stringify({
            id: p.id ?? null,
            name: p.name ?? null,
            production_branch: p.production_branch ?? null,
            production_script_name: p.production_script_name ?? null,
            preview_script_name: p.preview_script_name ?? null,
            uses_functions: p.uses_functions ?? null,
            build_config: p.build_config ?? null,
          }, null, 2));

          console.log("");
          console.log("=== PRODUCTION DEPLOYMENT CONFIG ===");
          console.log(JSON.stringify(summarize(production), null, 2));

          console.log("");
          console.log("=== PREVIEW DEPLOYMENT CONFIG ===");
          console.log(JSON.stringify(summarize(preview), null, 2));

          console.log("");
          console.log("=== RAW SERVICE BINDING CHECK ===");

          const productionServices = production.services ?? {};

          if (Object.keys(productionServices).length === 0) {
            console.log("NO PRODUCTION SERVICE BINDINGS FOUND");
          } else {
            console.log(JSON.stringify(
              services(productionServices),
              null,
              2,
            ));
          }

          console.log("");
          console.log("=== SECURITY NOTE ===");
          console.log(
            "Secret values are intentionally not printed."
          );
          NODE

      - name: Audit Cloudflare Pages projects
        run: |
          echo "=== CLOUDFLARE PAGES PROJECTS ==="
          npx wrangler pages project list

      - name: Audit Cloudflare Pages deployments
        run: |
          echo "=== CLOUDFLARE PAGES DEPLOYMENTS ==="
          npx wrangler pages deployment list \
            --project-name my-site


===== .github/workflows/cloudflare-worker-audit.yml =====

name: Cloudflare Worker Readonly Audit

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  audit-worker:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    env:
      CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_AUDIT_API_TOKEN }}
      CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
      WORKER_NAME: my-site
      EXPECTED_DB_NAME: my-site-db
      EXPECTED_DB_ID: 8495798c-ee01-40d2-95fd-70f58a6e1613
      EXPECTED_KV_ID: e38baa95fa0640498a127ba6d154671d

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Verify required secrets exist
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          if [ -z "${CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN:-}" ]; then
            echo "::error::Missing CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_AUDIT_API_TOKEN"
            exit 1
          fi

          if [ -z "${CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID:-}" ]; then
            echo "::error::Missing CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID"
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "Readonly audit credentials are present."

      - name: Audit Worker settings and bindings
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          API="https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4"
          AUTH_HEADER="Authorization: Bearer ${CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN}"

          echo "========================================"
          echo "1. Worker settings"
          echo "========================================"

          curl -fsS \
            -H "$AUTH_HEADER" \
            "$API/accounts/${CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID}/workers/scripts/${WORKER_NAME}/settings" \
            -o worker-settings.json

          jq '
            {
              success,
              result: {
                compatibility_date,
                compatibility_flags,
                usage_model,
                bindings: (
                  (.bindings // [])
                  | map(
                      {
                        name,
                        type,
                        id: (.id // null),
                        database_id: (.database_id // null),
                        namespace_id: (.namespace_id // null),
                        bucket_name: (.bucket_name // null),
                        service: (.service // null),
                        environment: (.environment // null),
                        secret_name: (.secret_name // null)
                      }
                    )
                ),
                assets: .assets,
                placement: .placement
              }
            }
          ' worker-settings.json

          echo
          echo "========================================"
          echo "2. Binding summary"
          echo "========================================"

          jq -r '
            (.result.bindings // [])
            | if length == 0 then
                "NO BINDINGS REPORTED"
              else
                .[]
                | [
                    (.name // "-"),
                    (.type // "-"),
                    (.id // .database_id // .namespace_id // .bucket_name // .service // "-"),
                    (.environment // "-")
                  ]
                | @tsv
              end
          ' worker-settings.json

          echo
          echo "========================================"
          echo "3. Secret / environment binding names"
          echo "========================================"

          jq -r '
            (.result.bindings // [])
            | map(select(.type == "secret_text" or .type == "secret_key" or .type == "plain_text"))
            | if length == 0 then
                "NO SECRET/ENV BINDINGS REPORTED"
              else
                .[]
                | [
                    (.name // "-"),
                    (.type // "-")
                  ]
                | @tsv
              end
          ' worker-settings.json

          echo
          echo "NOTE: Secret values are never printed."

      - name: Verify D1 database
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          API="https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4"
          AUTH_HEADER="Authorization: Bearer ${CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN}"

          echo
          echo "========================================"
          echo "4. Expected D1 database"
          echo "========================================"

          curl -fsS \
            -H "$AUTH_HEADER" \
            "$API/accounts/${CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID}/d1/database/${EXPECTED_DB_ID}" \
            -o d1.json

          jq '{
            success,
            result: {
              uuid,
              name,
              created_at,
              jurisdiction,
              file_size,
              num_tables,
              version,
              read_replication
            }
          }' d1.json

          DB_NAME="$(jq -r '.result.name // empty' d1.json)"
          DB_UUID="$(jq -r '.result.uuid // empty' d1.json)"

          if [ "$DB_NAME" != "$EXPECTED_DB_NAME" ]; then
            echo "::error::D1 name mismatch: expected ${EXPECTED_DB_NAME}, got ${DB_NAME}"
            exit 1
          fi

          if [ "$DB_UUID" != "$EXPECTED_DB_ID" ]; then
            echo "::error::D1 UUID mismatch"
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "D1 identity check: PASS"

      - name: Verify KV namespace
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          API="https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4"
          AUTH_HEADER="Authorization: Bearer ${CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN}"

          echo
          echo "========================================"
          echo "5. Expected KV namespace"
          echo "========================================"

          curl -fsS \
            -H "$AUTH_HEADER" \
            "$API/accounts/${CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID}/storage/kv/namespaces?per_page=100" \
            -o kv.json

          jq '{
            success,
            namespaces: [
              (.result // [])[]
              | {
                  id,
                  title,
                  jurisdiction,
                  supports_url_encoding
                }
            ],
            result_info
          }' kv.json

          KV_ID="$(jq -r --arg expected "$EXPECTED_KV_ID" '
            (.result // [])
            | map(select(.id == $expected))
            | if length == 1 then .[0].id else "" end
          ' kv.json)"

          if [ "$KV_ID" != "$EXPECTED_KV_ID" ]; then
            echo "::error::Expected KV namespace was not found"
            exit 1
          fi

          KV_TITLE="$(jq -r --arg expected "$EXPECTED_KV_ID" '
            (.result // [])
            | map(select(.id == $expected))
            | .[0].title // "-"
          ' kv.json)"

          echo "KV namespace identity: PASS"
          echo "KV title: ${KV_TITLE}"

      - name: Audit R2 buckets
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          API="https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4"
          AUTH_HEADER="Authorization: Bearer ${CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN}"

          echo
          echo "========================================"
          echo "6. R2 buckets"
          echo "========================================"

          R2_HTTP_CODE="$(curl -sS -o r2.json -w '%{http_code}' \
            -H "$AUTH_HEADER" \
            "$API/accounts/${CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID}/r2/buckets?per_page=100")"

          echo "R2 HTTP status: $R2_HTTP_CODE"

          if [ "$R2_HTTP_CODE" = "200" ]; then
            echo "R2 is enabled."
            jq '{
              success,
              buckets: [
                (.result.buckets // [])[]
                | {
                    name,
                    creation_date,
                    location,
                    jurisdiction,
                    storage_class
                  }
              ],
              result_info
            }' r2.json
          elif jq -e '.errors[]? | select(.code == 10042)' r2.json >/dev/null 2>&1; then
            echo "R2 is not enabled for this Cloudflare account."
            echo "R2 is optional for this project; continuing audit."
          else
            echo "R2 audit returned a non-fatal error:"
            jq '{success, errors, messages}' r2.json
            echo "R2 is optional for this project; continuing audit."
          fi


      - name: Audit Worker deployments
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          API="https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4"
          AUTH_HEADER="Authorization: Bearer ${CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN}"

          echo
          echo "========================================"
          echo "7. Worker deployments"
          echo "========================================"

          curl -fsS \
            -H "$AUTH_HEADER" \
            "$API/accounts/${CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID}/workers/scripts/${WORKER_NAME}/deployments" \
            -o deployments.json

          jq '
            {
              success,
              result: (
                (.result // [])
                | map({
                    id,
                    number,
                    created_on,
                    source,
                    annotations,
                    resources: {
                      bindings: (.resources.bindings // [] | map({
                        name,
                        type,
                        id: (.id // null),
                        database_id: (.database_id // null),
                        namespace_id: (.namespace_id // null),
                        bucket_name: (.bucket_name // null),
                        service: (.service // null),
                        environment: (.environment // null)
                      })),
                      script: {
                        etag: (.resources.script.etag // null),
                        handlers: (.resources.script.handlers // null),
                        last_deployed_from: (.resources.script.last_deployed_from // null)
                      },
                      script_runtime: {
                        compatibility_date: (.resources.script_runtime.compatibility_date // null),
                        compatibility_flags: (.resources.script_runtime.compatibility_flags // null)
                      }
                    }
                  })
              )
            }
          ' deployments.json

      - name: Audit result
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          echo
          echo "========================================"
          echo "READONLY WORKER AUDIT COMPLETE"
          echo "========================================"
          echo "Worker: ${WORKER_NAME}"
          echo "Expected D1: ${EXPECTED_DB_NAME}"
          echo "Expected KV: ${EXPECTED_KV_ID}"
          echo "R2: audited"
          echo "Secrets: names only, values never printed"
          echo "No Cloudflare resources were modified."


===== .github/workflows/d1-backup-release.yml =====

name: D1 Encrypted Backup

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
  schedule:
    - cron: "0 3 * * *"

permissions:
  contents: write

concurrency:
  group: d1-backup-release
  cancel-in-progress: false

env:
  DB_NAME: my-site-db
  DB_ID: 8495798c-ee01-40d2-95fd-70f58a6e1613

jobs:
  backup:
    name: Export, encrypt and release D1 backup
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 22
          cache: npm

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Verify required secrets
        env:
          CF_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
          CF_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
          BACKUP_KEY: ${{ secrets.BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          test -n "$CF_API_TOKEN"
          test -n "$CF_ACCOUNT_ID"
          test -n "$BACKUP_KEY"

          echo "Required backup secrets are configured."

      - name: Prepare backup workspace
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          rm -rf backup-work
          mkdir -p backup-work

          echo "Backup workspace prepared."

      - name: Export remote D1 via Cloudflare API
        env:
          CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
          CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
          DATABASE_ID: ${{ env.DB_ID }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          API_URL="https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/${CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID}/d1/database/${DATABASE_ID}/export"

          echo "Starting remote D1 export..."

          START_RESPONSE="$(
            curl \
              --fail \
              --silent \
              --show-error \
              --retry 3 \
              --retry-delay 2 \
              -X POST \
              "$API_URL" \
              -H "Authorization: Bearer ${CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN}" \
              -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
              --data '{"output_format":"polling"}'
          )"

          if ! echo "$START_RESPONSE" | jq -e '.success == true' >/dev/null; then
            echo "Cloudflare D1 export start failed."
            echo "$START_RESPONSE" | jq '{success,errors,messages,result}' || true
            exit 1
          fi

          BOOKMARK="$(echo "$START_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.result.at_bookmark // empty')"

          if [ -z "$BOOKMARK" ]; then
            echo "Cloudflare did not return an export bookmark."
            echo "$START_RESPONSE" | jq '{success,errors,messages,result}' || true
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "Remote D1 export started successfully."

          SIGNED_URL=""
          FILENAME=""

          for attempt in $(seq 1 120); do
            POLL_RESPONSE="$(
              curl \
                --fail \
                --silent \
                --show-error \
                --retry 3 \
                --retry-delay 2 \
                -X POST \
                "$API_URL" \
                -H "Authorization: Bearer ${CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN}" \
                -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
                --data "$(jq -nc --arg bookmark "$BOOKMARK" \
                  '{output_format:"polling",current_bookmark:$bookmark}')"
            )"

            if ! echo "$POLL_RESPONSE" | jq -e '.success == true' >/dev/null; then
              echo "Cloudflare D1 export polling failed."
              echo "$POLL_RESPONSE" | jq '{success,errors,messages,result}' || true
              exit 1
            fi

            STATUS="$(echo "$POLL_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.result.status // empty')"

            case "$STATUS" in
              complete)
                SIGNED_URL="$(echo "$POLL_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.result.result.signed_url // empty')"
                FILENAME="$(echo "$POLL_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.result.result.filename // empty')"

                if [ -z "$SIGNED_URL" ]; then
                  echo "Export completed but signed download URL is missing."
                  exit 1
                fi

                echo "Remote D1 export is complete."
                break
                ;;

              error)
                echo "Cloudflare reported a D1 export error."
                echo "$POLL_RESPONSE" | jq '{success,errors,messages,result}' || true
                exit 1
                ;;

              *)
                echo "Export still running (attempt ${attempt}/120)..."
                sleep 5
                ;;
            esac
          done

          if [ -z "$SIGNED_URL" ]; then
            echo "Timed out waiting for remote D1 export."
            exit 1
          fi

          if [ -z "$FILENAME" ]; then
            FILENAME="my-site.sql"
          fi

          echo "Downloading exported SQL..."

          curl \
            --fail \
            --silent \
            --show-error \
            --retry 5 \
            --retry-delay 2 \
            --retry-all-errors \
            -L \
            "$SIGNED_URL" \
            -o backup-work/my-site.sql

          test -s backup-work/my-site.sql

          echo "Remote D1 export downloaded successfully."
          echo "Cloudflare export filename: ${FILENAME}"
          echo "SQL file size: $(wc -c < backup-work/my-site.sql) bytes"

      - name: Validate exported D1
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          SQL_FILE="backup-work/my-site.sql"

          test -s "$SQL_FILE"

          grep -q 'd1_migrations' "$SQL_FILE"
          grep -q 'CREATE TABLE.*users' "$SQL_FILE"
          grep -q 'session_version' "$SQL_FILE"
          grep -q '0001_init.sql' "$SQL_FILE"
          grep -q '0002_security_session_version.sql' "$SQL_FILE"

          SQL_SHA256="$(sha256sum "$SQL_FILE" | awk '{print $1}')"

          echo "D1 export validation passed."
          echo "SQL SHA-256: ${SQL_SHA256}"

          echo "$SQL_SHA256" > backup-work/sql.sha256

      - name: Recalculate manifest safely
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          SQL_SHA256="$(cat backup-work/sql.sha256)"

          node - "$SQL_SHA256" "$DB_NAME" "$DB_ID" "$GITHUB_SHA" "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" <<'NODE'
          const fs = require("node:fs");

          const [
            ,

            ,
            sqlSha256,
            database,
            databaseId,
            commit,
            repository,
          ] = process.argv;

          const manifest = {
            format: "my-site-d1-backup-v1",
            database,
            database_id: databaseId,
            created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
            commit,
            repository,
            sql_sha256: sqlSha256,
          };

          fs.writeFileSync(
            "backup-work/manifest.json",
            JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2) + "\n"
          );
          NODE

          test -s backup-work/manifest.json

          echo "Backup manifest:"
          cat backup-work/manifest.json

      - name: Create backup archive
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          tar -czf backup-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz \
            -C backup-work \
            my-site.sql \
            manifest.json

          test -s backup-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz

          echo "Archive created."
          echo "Archive size: $(wc -c < backup-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz) bytes"

      - name: Encrypt backup
        env:
          BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY: ${{ secrets.BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY="$BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY" \
            node scripts/encrypt-backup.mjs \
            backup-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz \
            backup-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc

          test -s backup-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc

          sha256sum backup-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc \
            > backup-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc.sha256

          echo "Encrypted backup created."

      - name: Remove plaintext backup files
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          rm -f \
            backup-work/my-site.sql \
            backup-work/manifest.json \
            backup-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz \
            backup-work/sql.sha256

          if find backup-work -maxdepth 1 -type f \
            \( -name "*.sql" -o -name "*.tar.gz" -o -name "manifest.json" \) \
            | grep -q .; then
            echo "Plaintext backup material still exists."
            find backup-work -maxdepth 1 -type f -print
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "Plaintext backup material removed."

      - name: Create GitHub Release
        id: release
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          TAG="d1-backup-$(date -u +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"

          jq -n \
            --arg tag "$TAG" \
            --arg name "D1 Encrypted Backup $TAG" \
            --arg body "Automated encrypted D1 backup for ${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}." \
            '{
              tag_name: $tag,
              name: $name,
              body: $body,
              draft: false,
              prerelease: true,
              make_latest: "false"
            }' \
            > backup-work/release.json

          RELEASE_RESPONSE="$(
            curl \
              --fail \
              --silent \
              --show-error \
              -X POST \
              "https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/releases" \
              -H "Authorization: Bearer ${GH_TOKEN}" \
              -H "Accept: application/vnd.github+json" \
              -H "X-GitHub-Api-Version: 2022-11-28" \
              -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
              --data @backup-work/release.json
          )"

          RELEASE_ID="$(echo "$RELEASE_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.id // empty')"
          UPLOAD_URL="$(echo "$RELEASE_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.upload_url // empty' | sed 's/{?name,label}//')"

          if [ -z "$RELEASE_ID" ] || [ -z "$UPLOAD_URL" ]; then
            echo "Failed to create GitHub Release."
            echo "$RELEASE_RESPONSE" | jq '{message,errors}' || true
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "TAG=$TAG" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "RELEASE_ID=$RELEASE_ID" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "UPLOAD_URL=$UPLOAD_URL" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "GitHub Release created: $TAG"

      - name: Upload encrypted backup assets
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
          UPLOAD_URL: ${{ steps.release.outputs.UPLOAD_URL }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          upload_asset() {
            local file="$1"
            local content_type="$2"
            local name
            name="$(basename "$file")"

            curl \
              --fail \
              --silent \
              --show-error \
              --retry 3 \
              -X POST \
              "${UPLOAD_URL}?name=${name}" \
              -H "Authorization: Bearer ${GH_TOKEN}" \
              -H "Accept: application/vnd.github+json" \
              -H "X-GitHub-Api-Version: 2022-11-28" \
              -H "Content-Type: ${content_type}" \
              --data-binary "@${file}" \
              >/dev/null

            echo "Uploaded: ${name}"
          }

          upload_asset \
            backup-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc \
            application/octet-stream

          upload_asset \
            backup-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc.sha256 \
            text/plain

      - name: Verify GitHub Release assets
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
          TAG: ${{ steps.release.outputs.TAG }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          RELEASE_RESPONSE="$(
            curl \
              --fail \
              --silent \
              --show-error \
              "https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/releases/tags/${TAG}" \
              -H "Authorization: Bearer ${GH_TOKEN}" \
              -H "Accept: application/vnd.github+json" \
              -H "X-GitHub-Api-Version: 2022-11-28"
          )"

          echo "$RELEASE_RESPONSE" | jq -e '
            (.assets | map(.name) |
              index("my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc")) != null
          ' >/dev/null

          echo "$RELEASE_RESPONSE" | jq -e '
            (.assets | map(.name) |
              index("my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc.sha256")) != null
          ' >/dev/null

          if echo "$RELEASE_RESPONSE" | jq -e '
            .assets[].name |
            select(
              endswith(".sql") or
              endswith(".tar.gz") or
              endswith("manifest.json")
            )
          ' | grep -q .; then
            echo "Plaintext backup asset detected in GitHub Release."
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "GitHub Release assets verified."

      - name: Final cleanup
        if: always()
        run: |
          set +e

          rm -rf backup-work

          echo "Backup workspace cleaned."


===== .github/workflows/d1-inspect.yml =====

name: D1 Inspect

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  inspect:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10

    env:
      CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
      CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v6
        with:
          node-version: 22
          cache: npm

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Inspect D1 migration history
        run: |
          set -e
          npx wrangler d1 execute my-site-db --remote --command="SELECT * FROM d1_migrations ORDER BY id;"


===== .github/workflows/d1-readonly-capability-audit.yml =====

name: D1 Readonly Capability Audit

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  audit:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    env:
      CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
      CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 22
          cache: npm

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Verify Cloudflare token without exposing it
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          test -n "$CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN"
          test -n "$CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID"

          echo "Cloudflare token: present"
          echo "Account ID: present"

          HTTP_CODE="$(
            curl -sS -o token-verify.json -w '%{http_code}' \
              -H "Authorization: Bearer $CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN" \
              "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/user/tokens/verify"
          )"

          echo "Token verify HTTP status: $HTTP_CODE"

          jq '{
            success,
            result: (
              .result
              | {
                  id,
                  status,
                  not_before,
                  expires_on
                }
            ),
            errors,
            messages
          }' token-verify.json

          test "$HTTP_CODE" = "200"
          jq -e '.success == true' token-verify.json >/dev/null

      - name: Show Wrangler version
        run: npx wrangler --version

      - name: Show D1 command help
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          echo "========================================"
          echo "D1 help"
          echo "========================================"
          npx wrangler d1 --help

          echo
          echo "========================================"
          echo "D1 create help"
          echo "========================================"
          npx wrangler d1 create --help

          echo
          echo "========================================"
          echo "D1 delete help"
          echo "========================================"
          npx wrangler d1 delete --help

          echo
          echo "========================================"
          echo "D1 execute help"
          echo "========================================"
          npx wrangler d1 execute --help

          echo
          echo "========================================"
          echo "D1 export help"
          echo "========================================"
          npx wrangler d1 export --help

      - name: Readonly D1 list
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          HTTP_CODE="$(
            curl -sS -o d1-list.json -w '%{http_code}' \
              -H "Authorization: Bearer $CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN" \
              "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/${CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID}/d1/database?per_page=100"
          )"

          echo "D1 list HTTP status: $HTTP_CODE"

          jq '{
            success,
            databases: [
              (.result // [])[]
              | {
                  uuid,
                  name,
                  version,
                  created_at,
                  modified_at,
                  jurisdiction
                }
            ],
            result_info,
            errors,
            messages
          }' d1-list.json

          test "$HTTP_CODE" = "200"
          jq -e '.success == true' d1-list.json >/dev/null

      - name: Cleanup
        if: always()
        run: |
          rm -f token-verify.json d1-list.json


===== .github/workflows/d1-restore-test.yml =====

name: D1 Disaster Recovery Restore Test

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      backup_release_tag:
        description: "GitHub Release tag to restore-test, for example d1-backup-20260909-144051"
        required: true
        type: string

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: d1-restore-test
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  restore-test:
    name: Restore encrypted D1 backup into isolated temporary D1
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    env:
      CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
      CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
      BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY: ${{ secrets.BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY }}

      BACKUP_RELEASE_TAG: ${{ inputs.backup_release_tag }}
      DB_NAME: my-site-db
      PRODUCTION_DB_ID: 8495798c-ee01-40d2-95fd-70f58a6e1613

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 22
          cache: npm

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Verify required secrets and input
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          test -n "$CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN"
          test -n "$CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID"
          test -n "$BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY"
          test -n "$BACKUP_RELEASE_TAG"

          if ! [[ "$BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY" =~ ^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$ ]]; then
            echo "BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY must be exactly 64 hexadecimal characters."
            exit 1
          fi

          if ! [[ "$BACKUP_RELEASE_TAG" =~ ^d1-backup-[0-9]{8}-[0-9]{6}$ ]]; then
            echo "Invalid backup release tag format."
            echo "Expected: d1-backup-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS"
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "Required secrets are present."
          echo "Backup encryption key format: OK"
          echo "Backup release tag format: OK"

      - name: Prepare temporary workspace
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          mkdir -p backup-restore-work

          echo "Temporary workspace prepared."

      - name: Download encrypted backup
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          BASE_URL="https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/releases/download/${BACKUP_RELEASE_TAG}"

          curl -fL \
            -o backup-restore-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc \
            "${BASE_URL}/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc"

          curl -fL \
            -o backup-restore-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc.sha256 \
            "${BASE_URL}/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc.sha256"

          test -s backup-restore-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc
          test -s backup-restore-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc.sha256

          echo "Encrypted backup downloaded."

      - name: Verify encrypted backup SHA-256
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          (
            cd backup-restore-work
            echo "$(awk '{print $1}' my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc.sha256)  my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc" | sha256sum -c -
          )

          echo "Encrypted backup integrity: PASS"

      - name: Decrypt backup
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          node scripts/decrypt-backup.mjs \
            backup-restore-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz.enc \
            backup-restore-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz

          test -s backup-restore-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz

          echo "Backup decryption: PASS"

      - name: Extract backup
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          mkdir -p backup-restore-work/extracted

          tar -xzf \
            backup-restore-work/my-site-d1-backup.tar.gz \
            -C backup-restore-work/extracted

          test -s backup-restore-work/extracted/my-site.sql
          test -s backup-restore-work/extracted/manifest.json

          echo "Backup extraction: PASS"

          echo "Backup manifest:"
          jq '{
            format,
            database,
            created_at,
            commit,
            repository,
            sql_sha256
          }' backup-restore-work/extracted/manifest.json

      - name: Validate backup manifest
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          MANIFEST="backup-restore-work/extracted/manifest.json"
          SQL_FILE="backup-restore-work/extracted/my-site.sql"

          FORMAT="$(jq -r ".format // empty" "$MANIFEST")"
          DATABASE="$(jq -r ".database // empty" "$MANIFEST")"
          REPOSITORY="$(jq -r ".repository // empty" "$MANIFEST")"
          COMMIT_TYPE="$(jq -r ".commit | type" "$MANIFEST")"
          CREATED_AT_TYPE="$(jq -r ".created_at | type" "$MANIFEST")"
          SQL_SHA256_TYPE="$(jq -r ".sql_sha256 | type" "$MANIFEST")"
          EXPECTED_SQL_SHA256="$(jq -r ".sql_sha256 // empty" "$MANIFEST")"
          ACTUAL_SQL_SHA256="$(sha256sum "$SQL_FILE" | awk '{print $1}')"

          echo "Manifest format: $FORMAT"
          echo "Manifest database: $DATABASE"
          echo "Manifest repository: $REPOSITORY"
          echo "Manifest commit type: $COMMIT_TYPE"
          echo "Manifest created_at type: $CREATED_AT_TYPE"
          echo "Manifest sql_sha256 type: $SQL_SHA256_TYPE"
          echo "Expected SQL SHA-256: $EXPECTED_SQL_SHA256"
          echo "Actual SQL SHA-256:   $ACTUAL_SQL_SHA256"

          test "$FORMAT" = "my-site-d1-backup-v1"
          test "$DATABASE" = "$DB_NAME"
          test "$REPOSITORY" = "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY"
          test "$COMMIT_TYPE" = "string"
          test "$CREATED_AT_TYPE" = "string"
          test "$SQL_SHA256_TYPE" = "string"
          [[ "$EXPECTED_SQL_SHA256" =~ ^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$ ]]
          test "$EXPECTED_SQL_SHA256" = "$ACTUAL_SQL_SHA256"

          echo "Backup manifest validation: PASS"
          echo "SQL integrity validation: PASS"

      - name: Create isolated temporary D1
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          TEST_DB="my-site-restore-test-${GITHUB_RUN_ID}"

          if [ "$TEST_DB" = "$DB_NAME" ]; then
            echo "SAFETY FAILURE: temporary database name equals production database."
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "TEST_DB=$TEST_DB" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"

          CREATE_HTTP_CODE="$(
            curl -sS \
              -o backup-restore-work/create-d1.json \
              -w '%{http_code}' \
              -X POST \
              -H "Authorization: Bearer $CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN" \
              -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
              "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/${CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID}/d1/database" \
              -d "$(jq -n \
                --arg name "$TEST_DB" \
                '{
                  name: $name,
                  primary_location_hint: "apac",
                  read_replication: {
                    mode: "disabled"
                  }
                }')"
          )"

          echo "Create temporary D1 HTTP status: $CREATE_HTTP_CODE"

          jq '{
            success,
            result: (
              .result
              | {
                  name,
                  uuid,
                  version,
                  created_at,
                  jurisdiction,
                  read_replication
                }
            ),
            errors,
            messages
          }' backup-restore-work/create-d1.json

          test "$CREATE_HTTP_CODE" = "200"
          jq -e '.success == true' backup-restore-work/create-d1.json >/dev/null

          TEST_DB_ID="$(
            jq -r '.result.uuid' backup-restore-work/create-d1.json
          )"

          test "$TEST_DB_ID" != "null"
          test -n "$TEST_DB_ID"

          if [ "$TEST_DB_ID" = "$PRODUCTION_DB_ID" ]; then
            echo "SAFETY FAILURE: temporary D1 ID equals production D1."
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "TEST_DB_ID=$TEST_DB_ID" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"

          echo "Temporary D1 created successfully."
          echo "Temporary D1 name: $TEST_DB"
          echo "Production isolation check: PASS"

      - name: Import SQL into temporary D1
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          npx wrangler d1 execute "$TEST_DB" \
            --remote \
            --file=backup-restore-work/extracted/my-site.sql \
            --yes

          echo "D1 SQL restore: PASS"

      - name: Verify restored D1
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          echo "=== Migration count ==="

          npx wrangler d1 execute "$TEST_DB" \
            --remote \
            --command="SELECT COUNT(*) AS migration_count FROM d1_migrations;" \
            --json \
            > backup-restore-work/migration-count.json

          cat backup-restore-work/migration-count.json

          jq -e '
            (.[0].results[0].migration_count | type) == "number"
            and
            .[0].results[0].migration_count >= 2
          ' backup-restore-work/migration-count.json >/dev/null

          echo "Migration count: PASS (>= 2)"

          echo
          echo "=== Migration versions ==="

          npx wrangler d1 execute "$TEST_DB" \
            --remote \
            --command="SELECT id, name FROM d1_migrations ORDER BY id;" \
            --json \
            > backup-restore-work/migrations.json

          cat backup-restore-work/migrations.json

          jq -e '
            (
              [.[0].results[].name]
              | index("0001_init.sql") != null
            )
            and
            (
              [.[0].results[].name]
              | index("0002_security_session_version.sql") != null
            )
            and
            (
              [.[0].results[].name]
              | index("0003_add_user_role.sql") != null
            )
            and
            (
              [.[0].results[].name]
              | index("0004_add_user_profile.sql") != null
            )
          ' backup-restore-work/migrations.json >/dev/null

          echo "Migration versions: PASS (0001-0004)"

          echo
          echo "=== User count ==="

          npx wrangler d1 execute "$TEST_DB" \
            --remote \
            --command="SELECT COUNT(*) AS user_count FROM users;" \
            --json \
            > backup-restore-work/user-count.json

          cat backup-restore-work/user-count.json

          jq -e '
            (.[0].results[0].user_count | type) == "number"
            and
            .[0].results[0].user_count >= 0
          ' backup-restore-work/user-count.json >/dev/null

          echo "User table query: PASS"

          echo
          echo "=== Required schema ==="

          npx wrangler d1 execute "$TEST_DB" \
            --remote \
            --command="SELECT name, type FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name IN ('users','d1_migrations','idx_users_email','idx_users_username','idx_users_session_version') ORDER BY type,name;" \
            --json \
            > backup-restore-work/schema.json

          cat backup-restore-work/schema.json

          echo
          echo "=== Session version column ==="

          npx wrangler d1 execute "$TEST_DB" \
            --remote \
            --command="SELECT COUNT(*) AS session_version_column_count FROM pragma_table_info('users') WHERE name = 'session_version';" \
            --json \
            > backup-restore-work/session-version.json

          cat backup-restore-work/session-version.json

          jq -e '
            .[0].results[0].session_version_column_count == 1
          ' backup-restore-work/session-version.json >/dev/null

          echo "Session version column: PASS"

      - name: Assert restored D1 structure
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          jq -e '
            (
              [.[0].results[] | select(.name == "users" and .type == "table")]
              | length
            ) == 1
          ' backup-restore-work/schema.json >/dev/null

          jq -e '
            (
              [.[0].results[] | select(.name == "d1_migrations" and .type == "table")]
              | length
            ) == 1
          ' backup-restore-work/schema.json >/dev/null

          jq -e '
            (
              [.[0].results[] | select(.name == "idx_users_email" and .type == "index")]
              | length
            ) == 1
          ' backup-restore-work/schema.json >/dev/null

          jq -e '
            (
              [.[0].results[] | select(.name == "idx_users_username" and .type == "index")]
              | length
            ) == 1
          ' backup-restore-work/schema.json >/dev/null

          jq -e '
            (
              [.[0].results[] | select(.name == "idx_users_session_version" and .type == "index")]
              | length
            ) == 1
          ' backup-restore-work/schema.json >/dev/null

          jq -e '
            .[0].results[0].session_version_column_count == 1
          ' backup-restore-work/session-version.json >/dev/null

          echo "Required migration/schema checks: PASS"

      - name: Final restore verification
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          echo "========================================"
          echo "D1 DISASTER RECOVERY RESTORE TEST PASS"
          echo "========================================"
          echo "Backup release: $BACKUP_RELEASE_TAG"
          echo "Temporary database: $TEST_DB"
          echo
          echo "Verified:"
          echo "- Encrypted backup integrity"
          echo "- AES-256-GCM decryption"
          echo "- Backup archive extraction"
          echo "- Backup manifest"
          echo "- SQL SHA-256"
          echo "- D1 import"
          echo "- Migration count"
          echo "- Migration versions"
          echo "- users table"
          echo "- d1_migrations table"
          echo "- required indexes"
          echo "- session_version column"
          echo "- Production database isolation"

      - name: Cleanup temporary D1
        if: always()
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          if [ -z "${TEST_DB_ID:-}" ]; then
            echo "No temporary D1 ID recorded; nothing to delete."
            exit 0
          fi

          if [ "${TEST_DB_ID}" = "$PRODUCTION_DB_ID" ]; then
            echo "SAFETY FAILURE: refusing to delete production D1."
            exit 1
          fi

          DELETE_HTTP_CODE="$(
            curl -sS \
              -o backup-restore-work/delete-d1.json \
              -w '%{http_code}' \
              -X DELETE \
              -H "Authorization: Bearer $CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN" \
              "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/${CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID}/d1/database/${TEST_DB_ID}"
          )"

          echo "Delete temporary D1 HTTP status: $DELETE_HTTP_CODE"

          jq '{
            success,
            result,
            errors,
            messages
          }' backup-restore-work/delete-d1.json

          test "$DELETE_HTTP_CODE" = "200"
          jq -e '.success == true' backup-restore-work/delete-d1.json >/dev/null

          echo "Temporary D1 cleanup: PASS"

      - name: Final cleanup
        if: always()
        shell: bash
        run: |
          rm -rf backup-restore-work
          echo "Temporary restore workspace removed."

name: Deploy to Cloudflare Workers

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 20

    env:
      CLOUDFLARE_BUILD: "true"
      CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
      CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v6
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v6
        with:
          node-version: 22
          cache: npm

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Apply D1 migrations
        run: npx wrangler d1 migrations apply DB --remote

      - name: Build Astro
        run: npm run build

      - name: Deploy to Cloudflare Workers
        uses: cloudflare/wrangler-action@v3
        with:
          apiToken: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
          accountId: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
          command: deploy

name: Deploy to GitHub Pages

# 防止并发部署冲突：同一时间只允许一个部署，但不等前一个
# 完整执行完（cancel-in-progress: false 表示不取消进行中的，排队）
concurrency:
  group: "pages"
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - name: Remove API routes for static GitHub Pages build
        run: |
          set -e
          rm -rf src/pages/api
          rm -rf src/middleware.ts
          echo "API routes and server middleware removed for static build."

      - name: Install, build, and upload site
        uses: withastro/action@v6
        env:
          GITHUB_PAGES: "true"
          PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL: "https://my-site-n7j.pages.dev"

  deploy:
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    environment:
      name: github-pages
      url: ${{ steps.deployment.outputs.page_url }}

    steps:
      - name: Deploy to GitHub Pages
        id: deployment
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v5

## Development

When starting the dev server, use background mode:

```
astro dev --background
```

Manage the background server with `astro dev stop`, `astro dev status`, and `astro dev logs`.

import { defineConfig } from "astro/config";
import { unified } from "@astrojs/markdown-remark";
import react from "@astrojs/react";
import tailwindcss from "@tailwindcss/vite";

const isGitHubPages = process.env.GITHUB_PAGES === "true";
const isCloudflare = process.env.CLOUDFLARE_BUILD === "true";
const cloudflareWorkersModule = new URL("./src/lib/cloudflare-workers-stub.ts", import.meta.url).pathname;

const cloudflareAdapter = isCloudflare
  ? (await import("@astrojs/cloudflare")).default
  : undefined;

export default defineConfig({
  security: {
    checkOrigin: false,
  },
  ...(isGitHubPages
    ? {
        site: "https://ezuoju2-maker.github.io",
        base: "/my-site",
        output: "static",
      }
    : isCloudflare
      ? {
          output: "server",
          adapter: cloudflareAdapter(),
        }
      : {
          output: "static",
        }),
  markdown: {
    processor: unified(),
  },
  integrations: [react()],
  vite: {
    resolve: {
      alias: isCloudflare ? {} : { "cloudflare:workers": cloudflareWorkersModule },
    },
    plugins: [tailwindcss()],
  },
});


===== functions/api/[[path]].ts =====

interface Env {
  MY_SITE_WORKER: {
    fetch(request: Request): Promise<Response>;
  };
}

export const onRequest = async ({
  request,
  env,
}: {
  request: Request;
  env: Env;
}) => {
  const upstream = await env.MY_SITE_WORKER.fetch(request);

  // 手动重建响应，确保 set-cookie 头完整传递。
  //
  // Cloudflare Pages Functions 在 Service Binding 转发时，
  // 可能丢失或错误合并 set-cookie（这是已知坑）。
  // 这里用 getSetCookie() 逐个取出，再用 append 逐个写回。
  const newHeaders = new Headers();

  for (const [key, value] of upstream.headers.entries()) {
    if (key.toLowerCase() === "set-cookie") {
      continue;
    }
    newHeaders.set(key, value);
  }

  const response = new Response(upstream.body, {
    status: upstream.status,
    statusText: upstream.statusText,
    headers: newHeaders,
  });

  // getSetCookie() 是较新的 API，环境支持时优先使用
  const setCookies: string[] =
    typeof (upstream.headers as unknown as { getSetCookie?: () => string[] })
      .getSetCookie === "function"
      ? (
          upstream.headers as unknown as { getSetCookie: () => string[] }
        ).getSetCookie()
      : (() => {
          const single = upstream.headers.get("set-cookie");
          return single ? [single] : [];
        })();

  for (const cookie of setCookies) {
    response.headers.append("set-cookie", cookie);
  }

  return response;
};


===== migrations/0001_init.sql =====

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  username TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_email
  ON users(email);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_username
  ON users(username);


===== migrations/0002_security_session_version.sql =====

ALTER TABLE users
ADD COLUMN session_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_session_version
  ON users(id, session_version);


===== migrations/0003_add_user_role.sql =====

-- 0003_add_user_role.sql
--
-- 为 users 表添加 role 字段，用于区分普通用户和管理员。
-- 默认 'user'，管理员通过 SQL 手动提升。
--
-- 未来扩展：可以新增 'agent'（代理商）、'moderator'（客服）等值，
-- 应用层通过 src/lib/roles.ts 校验合法性。

ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN role TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user';

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_role ON users(role);


===== migrations/0004_add_user_profile.sql =====

-- 0004_add_user_profile.sql
--
-- 为 users 表添加个人资料字段：
-- - display_name: 显示昵称（默认等于 username，用户可改）
-- - avatar_url: 头像 URL（可选）
-- - bio: 个人简介（可选）

ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN display_name TEXT;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN avatar_url TEXT;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN bio TEXT;

===== README.md =====

# my-site

个人全栈网站：注册/登录/密码重置 + RBAC 权限。

## 在线入口

- 主站：https://my-site-n7j.pages.dev/
- 备用：https://ezuoju2-maker.github.io/my-site/

## 技术栈

- 前端：Astro + React + Tailwind CSS
- 后端：Cloudflare Workers + Astro SSR
- 数据：Cloudflare D1 (SQLite) + KV
- 邮件：Resend
- 人机验证：Cap（自托管 PoW）
- 部署：GitHub Actions

## 核心功能

- 用户注册（邮箱验证码 + Cap）
- 用户登录（记住登录 30 天）
- 忘记密码（邮箱验证码重置）
- RBAC 权限（user / admin）
- 用户中心 /dashboard/
- 管理后台 /admin/

## 本地开发

    npm install
    npm run dev

访问 http://localhost:4321

## 部署

推送 main 分支自动部署：

    git push origin main

## 日常维护

### 查看所有用户（Cloudflare D1 Console）

    SELECT id, username, email, role, created_at FROM users ORDER BY created_at DESC;

### 提升为管理员

    UPDATE users SET role = 'admin' WHERE username = '你的用户名';

### 清空所有账号

GitHub Actions -> Clear All Users (Manual Only) -> Run workflow -> 输入 YES-CLEAR-ALL

### 调试

URL 后加 ?debug=1 加载 Eruda 移动端 Console：

    https://my-site-n7j.pages.dev/?debug=1

## 项目结构

    src/
      components/           React 组件
      pages/                Astro 页面 + API 路由
      lib/                  工具库（auth/captcha/otp/cors/url 等）
      middleware.ts         安全响应头

## 安全特性

- PBKDF2-SHA256 10 万次迭代
- HttpOnly + Secure Session Cookie
- Cap PoW 人机验证
- Origin 白名单 CSRF 防护
- 登录/发码速率限制
- OTP 单次消费 + 独立签名密钥
- 安全响应头（nosniff / X-Frame / HSTS 等）


===== scripts/decrypt-backup.mjs =====

import fs from "node:fs";
import crypto from "node:crypto";

const [input, output] = process.argv.slice(2);

if (!input || !output) {
  console.error("Usage: node scripts/decrypt-backup.mjs <input> <output>");
  process.exit(2);
}

const secret = process.env.BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY;

if (!secret) {
  console.error("BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY is not configured");
  process.exit(2);
}

if (!/^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(secret)) {
  console.error("BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY must be exactly 64 hexadecimal characters");
  process.exit(2);
}

const key = Buffer.from(secret, "hex");
const data = fs.readFileSync(input);
const magic = Buffer.from("MY-SITE-BACKUP-V1\n", "utf8");

if (!data.subarray(0, magic.length).equals(magic)) {
  console.error("Invalid backup format");
  process.exit(1);
}

const offset = magic.length;
const iv = data.subarray(offset, offset + 12);
const authTag = data.subarray(offset + 12, offset + 28);
const ciphertext = data.subarray(offset + 28);

if (iv.length !== 12 || authTag.length !== 16 || ciphertext.length === 0) {
  console.error("Invalid encrypted backup structure");
  process.exit(1);
}

try {
  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);
  decipher.setAuthTag(authTag);

  const plaintext = Buffer.concat([
    decipher.update(ciphertext),
    decipher.final(),
  ]);

  fs.writeFileSync(output, plaintext);

  console.log(`Decrypted backup written: ${output}`);
  console.log(`Decrypted size: ${plaintext.length} bytes`);
} catch {
  console.error("Backup authentication failed");
  process.exit(1);
}


===== scripts/encrypt-backup.mjs =====

import fs from "node:fs";
import crypto from "node:crypto";

const [input, output] = process.argv.slice(2);

if (!input || !output) {
  console.error("Usage: node scripts/encrypt-backup.mjs <input> <output>");
  process.exit(2);
}

const secret = process.env.BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY;

if (!secret) {
  console.error("BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY is not configured");
  process.exit(2);
}

if (!/^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(secret)) {
  console.error("BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY must be exactly 64 hexadecimal characters");
  process.exit(2);
}

const key = Buffer.from(secret, "hex");
const plaintext = fs.readFileSync(input);

const iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);

const ciphertext = Buffer.concat([
  cipher.update(plaintext),
  cipher.final(),
]);

const authTag = cipher.getAuthTag();
const magic = Buffer.from("MY-SITE-BACKUP-V1\n", "utf8");

fs.writeFileSync(
  output,
  Buffer.concat([
    magic,
    iv,
    authTag,
    ciphertext,
  ]),
);

console.log(`Encrypted backup written: ${output}`);
console.log(`Encrypted size: ${fs.statSync(output).size} bytes`);


===== src/components/AdminDashboard.tsx =====

import { API_BASE_URL } from "../lib/api";
import { parseApiResponse } from "../lib/api-response";
import { getBase } from "../lib/url";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

type UserInfo = {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  role: string;
};

const getBasePath = getBase;

export default function AdminDashboard() {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<UserInfo | null>(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"loading" | "ok" | "denied">("loading");
  const [loggingOut, setLoggingOut] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function check() {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/me`, {
          method: "GET",
          credentials: "include",
          cache: "no-store",
        });

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (!response.ok) {
          window.location.replace(getBasePath());
          return;
        }

        const data = await parseApiResponse(response);

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (!data?.ok || !data.user) {
          window.location.replace(getBasePath());
          return;
        }

        // 非 admin 用户降级到用户中心
        if (data.user.role !== "admin") {
          window.location.replace(`${getBasePath()}dashboard/`);
          return;
        }

        setUser(data.user as UserInfo);
        setStatus("ok");
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) {
          window.location.replace(getBasePath());
        }
      }
    }

    void check();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  async function handleLogout() {
    if (loggingOut) return;
    setLoggingOut(true);

    try {
      await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/logout`, {
        method: "POST",
        credentials: "include",
      });
    } catch {
      // 忽略登出网络错误
    }

    // 兜底：清掉手动设置的 Cookie
    document.cookie = "session=; Path=/; Max-Age=0; Secure; SameSite=Lax";

    window.location.replace(getBasePath());
  }

  if (status === "loading" || !user) {
    return (
      <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-neutral-50">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="mx-auto h-8 w-8 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-neutral-300 border-t-neutral-900" />
          <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-neutral-500">正在验证管理员权限…</p>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const initial = user.username.charAt(0).toUpperCase();

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-neutral-950 text-neutral-100">
      {/* 顶部导航栏 */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-10 border-b border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900">
        <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-6xl items-center justify-between px-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-neutral-700 bg-neutral-950 text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-300">
              LOGO
            </div>
            <span className="text-base font-medium text-neutral-100">
              管理后台
            </span>
            <span className="rounded-md border border-red-500/40 bg-red-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-red-300">
              ADMIN
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-neutral-100 text-sm font-medium text-neutral-900">
              {initial}
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleLogout}
              disabled={loggingOut}
              className="rounded-lg border border-neutral-700 bg-neutral-800 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loggingOut ? "退出中…" : "退出"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* 主内容 */}
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-5 py-10">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-neutral-100">
          管理员控制台，{user.username}
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-neutral-400">{user.email}</p>

        <div className="mt-10 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900 p-8">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-neutral-800 text-2xl">
              🚧
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-neutral-100">
                管理后台正在开发中
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-neutral-400">
                我们正在为您构建以下功能：
              </p>
              <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2 text-sm text-neutral-200">
                <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-neutral-500">•</span>
                  商品管理
                </li>
                <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-neutral-500">•</span>
                  订单管理
                </li>
                <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-neutral-500">•</span>
                  用户管理
                </li>
                <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-neutral-500">•</span>
                  数据统计与报表
                </li>
                <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-neutral-500">•</span>
                  系统设置
                </li>
              </ul>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}


===== src/components/AutoLoginRedirect.tsx =====

import { API_BASE_URL } from "../lib/api";
import { parseApiResponse } from "../lib/api-response";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { withBase } from "../lib/url";


export default function AutoLoginRedirect() {
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function checkSession() {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(
          `${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/me`,
          {
            method: "GET",
            credentials: "include",
            cache: "no-store",
          },
        );

        if (!cancelled && response.ok) {
          const data = await parseApiResponse(response);
          const role = data?.user?.role === "admin" ? "admin" : "user";
          const target = role === "admin" ? "/admin/" : "/dashboard/";
          window.location.replace(
            withBase(target),
          );
        }
      } catch {
        // Ignore network errors and leave the login page available.
      }
    }

    void checkSession();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  return null;
}


===== src/components/CapWidget.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { CAP_SCRIPT_URL, CAP_API_ENDPOINT } from "../lib/cap-config";
import { useTranslation } from "../i18n/useTranslation";

type Props = {
  onSolve: (token: string) => void;
  onReset?: () => void;
};

let capScriptLoading: Promise<void> | null = null;

function loadCapScript(): Promise<void> {
  if (customElements.get("cap-widget")) return Promise.resolve();
  if (capScriptLoading) return capScriptLoading;

  capScriptLoading = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const existing = document.querySelector<HTMLScriptElement>(
      'script[data-cap-script="true"]',
    );
    if (existing) {
      // 上次已插入的脚本可能执行失败，删除后重新加载
      existing.remove();
    }
    const script = document.createElement("script");
    script.src = CAP_SCRIPT_URL;
    script.async = true;
    script.setAttribute("data-cap-script", "true");
    script.onload = () => {
      if (customElements.get("cap-widget")) {
        resolve();
        return;
      }
      // 脚本已加载但未注册 customElement，等最多 5 秒
      const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        capScriptLoading = null;
        reject(new Error("cap-widget not registered within 5s"));
      }, 5000);
      customElements
        .whenDefined("cap-widget")
        .then(() => {
          window.clearTimeout(timer);
          resolve();
        })
        .catch((err) => {
          window.clearTimeout(timer);
          capScriptLoading = null;
          reject(err);
        });
    };
    script.onerror = () => {
      capScriptLoading = null;
      reject(new Error("Failed to load cap.min.js"));
    };
    document.head.appendChild(script);
  });

  return capScriptLoading;
}

/**
 * 递归遍历 light DOM + shadow DOM 的所有文本节点。
 * Cap 组件用 Shadow DOM，必须递归进去才能改文本。
 */
function walkAllText(root: Node, callback: (textNode: Text) => void) {
  if (root.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
    callback(root as Text);
    return;
  }
  if (
    root.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE &&
    root.nodeType !== Node.DOCUMENT_FRAGMENT_NODE
  ) {
    return;
  }
  const el = root as Element;
  if (el.shadowRoot) {
    walkAllText(el.shadowRoot, callback);
  }
  const children = root.childNodes;
  for (let i = 0; i < children.length; i += 1) {
    walkAllText(children[i], callback);
  }
}

function translateCapWidget(root: Element, labels: Record<string, string>) {
  walkAllText(root, (textNode) => {
    const text = textNode.nodeValue ?? "";
    if (!text) return;
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(labels)) {
      if (!key) continue;
      if (text.includes(key)) {
        textNode.nodeValue = text.split(key).join(value);
        break;
      }
    }
  });
}

export default function CapWidget({ onSolve, onReset }: Props) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const onSolveRef = useRef(onSolve);
  const onResetRef = useRef(onReset);
  onSolveRef.current = onSolve;
  onResetRef.current = onReset;

  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const labelsRef = useRef<Record<string, string>>({});
  labelsRef.current = {
    "Verify to continue": t("cap.verify"),
    Verified: t("cap.verified"),
    Verifying: t("cap.verifying"),
    "点击进行人机验证": t("cap.verify"),
    已验证: t("cap.verified"),
    验证中: t("cap.verifying"),
    "Cap-Worker": "",
  };

  // 挂载 widget（只跑一次）
  useEffect(() => {
    const container = containerRef.current;
    if (!container) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    let widget: HTMLElement | null = null;
    let observer: MutationObserver | null = null;
    let intervalId: number | null = null;

    const translate = () => {
      if (!widget) return;
      translateCapWidget(widget, labelsRef.current);
    };

    const handleSolve = (event: Event) => {
      const detail = (event as CustomEvent<{ token?: string }>).detail;
      if (detail?.token) onSolveRef.current(detail.token);
      window.setTimeout(translate, 30);
      window.setTimeout(translate, 120);
    };
    const handleReset = () => {
      onResetRef.current?.();
      window.setTimeout(translate, 30);
      window.setTimeout(translate, 120);
    };

    loadCapScript()
      .then(() => {
        if (cancelled || !container) return;
        if (container.querySelector("cap-widget")) return;

        widget = document.createElement("cap-widget");
        widget.setAttribute("data-cap-api-endpoint", CAP_API_ENDPOINT);
        widget.addEventListener("solve", handleSolve);
        widget.addEventListener("reset", handleReset);
        container.appendChild(widget);

        // 立即翻译
        translate();
        window.setTimeout(translate, 50);
        window.setTimeout(translate, 200);

        // Observer：cap-widget + shadow root
        observer = new MutationObserver(translate);
        const targets: (Element | ShadowRoot)[] = [widget];
        if (widget.shadowRoot) targets.push(widget.shadowRoot);
        for (const target of targets) {
          observer.observe(target, {
            childList: true,
            subtree: true,
            characterData: true,
          });
        }

        // 兜底轮询：8 秒内每 400ms 一次
        let ticks = 0;
        intervalId = window.setInterval(() => {
          translate();
          ticks += 1;
          if (ticks >= 20) {
            if (intervalId !== null) window.clearInterval(intervalId);
            intervalId = null;
          }
        }, 400);
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        console.error("[CapWidget] failed to load cap.min.js", error);
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      if (observer) observer.disconnect();
      if (intervalId !== null) window.clearInterval(intervalId);
      if (widget) {
        widget.removeEventListener("solve", handleSolve);
        widget.removeEventListener("reset", handleReset);
        widget.remove();
      }
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  // 语言变化时立即翻译（不重建 widget）
  useEffect(() => {
    const widget = containerRef.current?.querySelector(
      "cap-widget",
    ) as HTMLElement | null;
    if (widget) {
      translateCapWidget(widget, labelsRef.current);
    }
  }, [t]);

  return <div ref={containerRef} className="flex justify-center" />;
}
